- **Role:** Ad-Fraud-Bot-Traffic Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Programmatic Advertising CTR Invalid Traffic Fraud

- **Key tolerance:** Ambient temperature must be 20°C ± 2°C. Relative humidity must be 45% ± 5%. Network latency between workstation and ad server must be ≤ 5 ms.
